### Step 0: existing work (no working, downloadable mod does what we want)
1. **Volpestyle/kh2-multiplayer** (github.com/Volpestyle/kh2-multiplayer). 98 commits, 2026-03-23 → 2026-10-02, very active. C++ inject DLL (MinHook) + runtime process + ENet relay server. Status per its own docs: friend actor walk/run under player control (no attack/jump/guard); three instances on one PC; avatars + shared enemy HP/deaths on loopback; host-led room following passed 20 loads. Internet test only against the host's own Tailscale address. **No LICENSE file** → reading for reference is fine, copying code needs the author's permission. Excellent docs: `docs/pointer_map_v1.md`, `LESSONS_LEARNED.md`, `ONLINE_COOP_PLAN.md`, `INPUT_RE_SESSION.md`, `research/PRIOR_ART.md`. Targets "1.0.0.10 Steam Global". Our exe still needs checking against it.
2. **Expert595/kh2-multiplayer** (github.com/Expert595/kh2-multiplayer). 2026-09-05, 4 commits, downloadable. Python client + TCP/JSON relay. Movement + independent rooms only, no combat sync. Experimental "native Sora/Roxas replica in a companion slot" (local two-instance only), done by writing the playable-character selector into the world party table before a room load. Warns: never save while replica slot active. No license.
3. **Snackya/KH2-co-op-mix** (2021, MIT). Randomizer "shared-hub" item/level sync via GoA. Broken on PC ≥1.0.0.8. Not a character co-op; little to reuse.
4. **kh-vids "Kingdom Hearts II Multiplayer Hack"** (PS2, codes). Old; second player moves/attacks on PS2 PAL; FM version movement-only. Proves friend control is possible at all.
5. **Player-class Roxas mods**: Playable Dual Wield Roxas (nexusmods kingdomhearts2finalmix/mods/368), Roxas Mod by Napstio (nexus mods/18, GitHub Napstio/Roxas-Mod). Both make Roxas the *player* (slot 0). Relevant if P2 is the local player on their own PC.

### Key design insight (needs user decision)
Both co-op projects independently moved to a "local-primary" model: each human is the native player (slot 0) on their own PC, and the other human appears as a puppet in a party slot. Reason: making a friend/AI actor accept full controller input (attack, jump, guard) is the hardest RE problem; Volpestyle has movement only after months. The user's brief (P2 = friend-slot Roxas driven by a 2nd controller) is the hard path. See MODDING_PLAN.md §3.

### Next step
- Waiting on the user: architecture choice (MODDING_PLAN.md §3), whether to contact Volpestyle, open design questions (§6).
- Then: confirm our exe matches Volpestyle's target build (compare code bytes at a documented hook site while the game runs).

### Decisions (user, 2026-10-04)
- D1 Architecture: **Option A, local-primary.** Each player is slot 0 on their own PC; P2 = Roxas-model player on P2's PC; on host, P2 is a puppet in a locked party slot.
- D2 Prior work: **build our own**, Volpestyle/Expert595 docs used only as leads, re-verified on our build. No code copied. *(Changed in session 15, 2026-10-08: Volpestyle is now GPL like us, so their code may be ported with credit.)*
- D3 §6 proposals accepted: drive form absorbs P2 (hidden/invuln/spectate); cutscenes = P2 holds behind overlay then resync; host pause freezes P2; P2 menu local + invuln; minigames/Gummi = P2 spectates; P2 KO = downed 20 s or revived by Sora's Cure/item, never game over; Sora death = normal game over, P2 follows.

### Slice 1: our DLL inside the game (2026-10-04)
- **Loading route (verified in source + installed binary):** OpenKH Panacea `LoadDLLs()` loads every `*.dll` in `<mod_path>\kh2\dll\` (here `D:\SteamLibrary\steamapps\common\KINGDOM HEARTS openkh\mod\kh2\dll\`), then calls exported `OnInit(const wchar_t* modFolder)` once and `OnFrame()` every game frame from its hook on the app vtable's onFrame (game thread). Source: OpenKh.Research.Panacea/Panacea.cpp (OpenKH main, 2026-08-26). Installed DBGHELP.dll contains "Loaded DLL", "OnInit", "OnFrame" strings.
- Caveat: `mod\kh2\` is generated by Mods Manager; a "Build" there may wipe `dll\`. Dev: copy by hand. Later: ship the DLL as a copy asset in an OpenKH mod.
- `src/dll/kh2coop.cpp` + `CMakeLists.txt` (VS 2022 generator, x64, static CRT). Built clean, deployed to the dll folder. Read-only: logs world/room/door + Sora/Donald/Goofy HP every 60 frames to `kh2coop.log` next to the DLL.
- Build: `cmake -S . -B build -G "Visual Studio 17 2022" -A x64 && cmake --build build --config Release` (cmake from VS BuildTools path above).
- Pending: user restarts game, confirms the log fills and the game behaves normally.

### Enabled OpenKH mods review (2026-10-04)
- Asset-only (keep): Texture_HD_KH2-4.0, TritonFix (1 obj + 1 menu texture), SpanishVoices130, EngVoicesFinalMix120.

Atlantica Button Fix writes menu slots only in world 0x0B (keep). **Treasure Magnet Plus writes byte 0x75 to code at exe+0x3DC56A every frame** (same code area as damage functions 0x3D2EB0..0x3E7C30): asked user to disable during development.
- Steam-detect string used by KSX scripts: u64 at exe+0x585E59 == 0x7265737563697065 ("epicuser" as little-endian ASCII); they check it at a different address per build. Not needed by us.

User must tick "KH2 Coop" in Mods Manager once.

Log matched the user's play exactly: title (world/room 0xFF, HP 0/0) → save loaded in Merlin's House (04/0D, **door 0x63 when spawning from a save**) → out to 04/09 door 02 → hit, Sora 28→16 → back in 04/0D door 00 → HP back to 28 (save point heal). Game unaffected.
- Log was locked while game ran (_wfopen_s denies sharing). Fixed: `_wfsopen(..., _SH_DENYNO)`; redeployed after game closed.

### Slice 2: two copies on one PC (2026-10-04)
- **Launching extra copies:** starting the exe directly makes steam_api restart it through Steam (opens the collection launcher). Starting it with env `SteamAppId=2552430` + `SteamGameId=2552430` (Steam running, game owned) runs it directly; two copies ran fine (~700-800 MB each), each loaded our DLL (per-PID log `kh2coop_<pid>.log`).
- **Pad mapping (Steam Input on, "default settings"; Xbox pad + PS4 pad over USB):** Xbox pad shows up on XInput slots **0 and 1 at once** (real pad + Steam virtual duplicate); PS4 pad on slot **2** (Steam virtual XInput). Both copies see all pads, focused or not.
- **Game input gate:** user saw both copies act on input only when focused, though XInput reported presses to both. Imports: `XINPUT1_4.dll` ordinals 2 (XInputGetState) and 3 (XInputSetState), `USER32!GetForegroundWindow` (IAT rva 0x57B518), `DINPUT8!DirectInput8Create`, steam_api64 (no ISteamInput-specific import beyond FindOrCreateUserInterface). Hypothesis: focus gate uses GetForegroundWindow.
- **Slice 2b:** IAT hooks, env-gated: `KH2COOP_PAD=n` → game slot 0 = XInput slot n, others disconnected; `KH2COOP_BACKGROUND=1` → GetForegroundWindow returns the game's own window. Copy A pid 2524 (PAD=0, Xbox), copy B pid 9232 (PAD=2, PS4), both hooked OK. Pending user test.
- `tools/pe_imports.py`: read-only import table lister.
- First 2b test inconclusive: copies opened fullscreen (I'd force-killed the previous copies, so the in-game windowed setting wasn't kept) and the PS4 pad produced no XInput input at all (faulty USB cable, since replaced).
- Windowed setting: set in-game; the game wrote `KH1.5+2.5_system_WW.png` (system save) at that moment, not `config1525.dat`. Monitors: primary 2560x1440 at (0,0), plus 1920x1080 above and 2560x1440 left.
- `tools/launch_pair.py`: launches copy A (KH2COOP_PAD=0) and B (PAD=2), both BACKGROUND=1, places them at 1280x760 side by side on the primary. Run 17:45: A pid 24676 (left), B pid 20104 (right), both windowed at title.
- Diagnostics added: game calls our XInputGetState hook ~60/s (≈1 call per frame); IAT swap stays ours (Steam doesn't overwrite it).
- **Test 17:45 result:** XInput filter worked at its level (copy B's filtered view saw only slot 2), but the game still took both pads, and only while *a KH2 window* was foreground (nothing while the terminal was focused). Unfocused copy B got movement + odd button meanings, no camera. Conclusion: KH2 also reads pads via **Steam Input API** (asks steam_api for "SteamInput006" via SteamInternal_FindOrCreateUserInterface; Volpestyle: enumeration FUN_140133970 binds pads as XInput or Steam Input, flag at gamepad+0x4C). Steam Input only feeds the foreground Steam game window.
- **Dead end:** returning null for "SteamInput006" → copy crashed instantly (0xC0000005); the game never null-checks the interface.
- **Verified ISteamInput006 vtable slots** from the game's own steam_api64.dll flat wrappers (`mov rax,[rcx]; jmp [rax+off]`): Init 0x00, RunFrame 0x18, GetConnectedControllers 0x30, GetInputTypeForHandle 0x128, GetControllerForGamepadIndex 0x130, GetGamepadIndexForController 0x138. 48 flat ISteamInput functions.
- **Slice 2c:** keep the real ISteamInput object, swap its vptr to a copied 64-entry vtable where GetConnectedControllers → 0 and GetControllerForGamepadIndex → 0. Both copies start fine (17:57, A pid 2832 left, B pid 23924 right). Pending user test.
- Tools: `tools/disasm.py` (capstone, read-only), `tools/xrefs.py` (direct call/jmp xrefs; found none for GetSteamInput accessor 0x14D380).
- **XInput slot order is not stable:** after the PS4 cable was reconnected, PS4 = slot 0 and Xbox = slots 1+2; next session Xbox = 0+1 and PS4 = 2 again. Pad assignment by slot number needs checking every launch (todo: make it stable).
- **Raw-slot logging (read-only)** of KH2's input struct (*(exe+0x79CF00)): controller count reads 4; raw slot 0 = active controller after the collector's swap; `+0x129C` active raw index went 0→2 when the PS4 pad was used, in BOTH copies, although XInput only exposed one pad per copy → a third route.
- **Third route = DirectInput game controllers.** Proof: KH2's DXInput init calls `IDirectInput8::EnumDevices` (vtable +0x20, index 4 per Windows SDK 10.0.22621 dinput.h) with edx=4 = `DI8DEVCLASS_GAMECTRL` at exe+0x133642. Symptoms matched (PS4 in the unfocused copy: no camera, shuffled face buttons; exclusive acquisition probably made the 18:03 split look "almost flawless").
- **Slice 2d:** `KH2COOP_XINPUT_ONLY=1` now also hooks `DINPUT8!DirectInput8Create` and gives the object a copied vtable whose EnumDevices hides non-keyboard/mouse devices. Log at 18:11 shows 3 × "Controller (XBOX 360 For Windows)" type 0x15 hidden (Steam's virtual pads + real Xbox), re-enumerated about every second (log spam; throttle later). A pid 15776 (slot 0, Xbox), B pid 18968 (slot 2, PS4). Pending user test.
- **Hiding DirectInput pads = dead end:** both pads stopped working in both copies; the game then called XInputGetState **0 times**. KH2 discovers pads via DirectInput enumeration first, then reads XInput-capable ones through XInput. Don't hide them.
- **Verified XInput → KH2 raw button bits** (paired log lines, PS2 DualShock order): Back→0x0001 (Select), R-thumb→0x0004 (R3), Start→0x0008, DPad Down→0x0040, LB→0x0400 (L1), Y?→0x1000 (pending), B→0x2000 (Circle), A→0x4000 (Cross), X→0x8000 (Square). Raw slot 1 (DirectInput route) had shifted bits (A→0x8000, B→0x4000), which explains the PS4 shuffle.
- **Slice 2e (robust approach):** MinHook v1.3.4 (FetchContent, BSD-2) inline hook on the input collector exe+0x105810 (prologue `4C 8B DC 56 48 81 EC F0 00 00 00` checked before hooking). After the original runs: raw slot 0 = assigned XInput pad (read by our DLL, mapped to PS2 bits, sticks to 0..255 with 0x80 centre and Y inverted, XInput deadzones), raw slots 1-7 neutral. Launcher default `KH2COOP_XINPUT_ONLY=0` now. 18:18: A pid 12032 (slot 0), B pid 17216 (slot 2), both "input override: hooked". Pending user test.
- **Slice 2 VERIFIED by user (2026-10-04 18:20):** left copy obeys only the Xbox pad, right copy only the PS4 pad, regardless of which KH2 window is focused; no cross-talk. Both loaded the HB save (world 04 room 0D). Buttons exercised in logs: A, B, X, Y, D-pad up/down, LB, combos; user reports everything behaves as expected. Not yet exercised: D-pad left/right, LT/RT, RB, L3.
- Known limitation (accepted by user): with a non-KH2 window focused (e.g. the terminal) neither copy reacts, even with the override. Not needed: in real play the game window is focused.
- One-PC test recipe: Steam running → `py tools/launch_pair.py --pad-a <xbox slot> --pad-b <ps4 slot>`; check slot order in the "pad N buttons" log lines (currently Xbox = 0+1, PS4 = 2).

### Slice 3b: Donald puppet (2026-10-04)
- Verified live: `exe+0x2A239B0` → Donald's actor (model P_EX020), `exe+0x2A239B8` → Goofy (P_EX030); both next to Sora, team 1 (actor+0x4DC). Actors live inside the exe data section (Sora at exe+0x2510EE0, Donald exe+0x252BB50, Goofy exe+0x253D8C0 in HB 04/09).
- Function starts checked (int3 padding before each): PerEntityUpdate 0x3BFD30 `40 53 48 83 EC 30 48 8B D9`; FriendAI thunk 0x1B0020 `mov rcx,rdx; jmp 0x3C3660`; MotionChainSet 0x3C88C0; SetMotion 0x3C86A0.
- `src/dll/puppet.{hpp,cpp}` (own code; technique from Volpestyle's Session 5 notes): when the peer's pose is fresh (≤120 frames) and in our world+room (and not mid-load at 0,0,0), Donald's friend AI is skipped, his motion is set via SetMotion(actor+0x158, motion, 0, 0) only on change (MotionChainSet blocked for him except our own call), and after his PerEntityUpdate we write pos/angle/sin/cos, zero velocity (+0xB98), carried displacement (+0x690), accel (+0xA48, 0x18 bytes), hold follow timer (+0xBA8 = 999) and set no-collide (actor+0x18C |= 0x40, restored on release). Motions > 8 → fall if airborne else idle.
- `HookFunction()` in common: byte-checked MinHook helper (input override uses it too). Env `KH2COOP_PUPPET=1` (launcher default on).
- 18:42 launch: A pid 6108, B pid 13640; all 4 hooks ok in both. Pending user test.
- **Slice 3b VERIFIED (18:44-18:49):** user: in each copy Donald moved with the other copy's pad (walk, run, jump), no crash or jitter; ~23k packets, 0 lost. Donald actor in Merlin's House = exe+0x2516E20. Not exercised: release on leaving the room.
- **Slice 3c (Roxas look):** object table found at exe+0x2A254D0 (VERIFIED_OFFSETS.md). `KH2COOP_PUPPET_ROXAS=1`: once the table is loaded, Donald's row (id 92) gets Roxas's model + motion set names (P_EX110 / P_EX110.mset), checked by id and expected names first. While patched, Donald's friend AI never runs (idle hold when not driven); all motions except 9 are passed through (Roxas's set has Sora-class moves). 18:53 launch: A pid 19148, B pid 26948, both "object table patched" at the title screen. Pending user test (load save → Donald should spawn as Roxas).

The HUD party portrait switched to Roxas (drawn too big). - Fix (19:04 launch, A pid 28228, B pid 29080): all 81 W_EX020* rows renamed to W_EX010 (+ same world variant); puppet motions now an allow-list (0-6, 41, 151, 153, 154, 161, 164, 173, 181), others → idle/fall. Pending test.

Keyblade attached at Roxas's head (Donald's weapon joint 2 used on Roxas's skeleton). Different-room behaviour as designed: puppet idles in place, resumes copying when both are in the same room. User's "doesn't despawn" meant the keyblade staying visible, which is fine; the problem was where it sits.
- Fix (19:12, A pid 19256, B pid 17184): Donald's row byte 7 (weapon joint) = Roxas's 0x0E. Pending test.
- **Weapon joint result (19:14, user + 2 screenshots):** keyblade now in Roxas's hand, but held backwards during attacks and the keychain floats with no physics. Hypothesis: friend-class (party member) weapon handling, not Roxas. Real player weapon behaviour likely needs a *player-class* actor in the party slot (Expert595/Volpestyle "Sora clone" via the save's world party table at save+0x3534 + world*4).
- User suggested a Sora clone instead. Quick test of the hypothesis: `KH2COOP_PUPPET_LOOK=sora` patches Donald's row with Sora's row (id 84): P_EX100 / P_EX100.mset, weapon joint **1** (Sora's byte 7), all motions except 9 allowed. 19:21: A pid 22400, B pid 18224. Pending test.
- **Sora look = crash (19:22):** both copies crashed during the save's room load (log: world 04/0D set, party HP still 0/0, then nothing). The game's own CrashDump.dmp was 0 bytes. A friend-class actor can't be built from Sora's objentry data (P_EX100 / P_EX100.mset / joint 1), though Roxas's works. Dead end for the objentry route; launcher default stays `KH2COOP_PUPPET_LOOK=roxas`. Sora clones need the player-class route (party table).
- **Player-class route (user chose it):** `KH2COOP_PUPPET_LOOK=clone`. Save base: check "KH2J" at exe+0x9A98B0, then 0x9A9830. Party table save+0x3534+4*world (lead: Volpestyle pointer map + OpenKH WorldPartyMembers + Expert595): if the current world's entry is {00 player, 01 Donald, ...}, set companion 1 = 00 (a copy of the playable character; Donald benched). Clone = any player-class actor (objentry type byte 0) other than the list-head Sora, collected in the PerEntityUpdate hook; driven post-update (motion all except 9, transform; no follow timer). Clone appears from the next room load. **Saving while active would persist the party change.**
- **Clone result (19:28, user):** the Sora copy spawned straight away on save load (party patch lands before the room's party spawns); walk, run, jump, attacks and spells all correct on the puppet, keyblade fine. Two problems: (1) copy B crashed when its player opened the pause menu (Options) while the clone was driven; log: menu stopped entity updates → my code saw no clone → "Sora copy gone" → released → crash. Copy A opened its menu fine with an undriven clone. (2) An undriven clone obeys the local controller like Sora ("I control both Soras").
- Fixes (19:34, A pid 14900, B pid 15844): `src/dll/crash_log.cpp`, a vectored exception handler logging code/RIP (module+RVA)/registers/stack return RVAs for faults outside our DLL (cap 20); frames with no entity updates no longer count as "clone gone"; undriven clone is held: frozen idle at its spot (transform rewritten every update, motion changes blocked).

Crash logger fired: AV reading 0x1EC601BE214 (heap), rbx=FFFFFFFF, rax=7FFFFFE; but the RVA printed as "0xlX" (wsprintfA has no %llX). Fixed with snprintf + fault bytes. With the clone in the party, stat slot k=1 below Sora held Goofy's 30/30 and k=2 0/0: **stat slots follow party position**, not character.

Pausing one copy freezes its Sora in the other copy (mid-air if jumping); expected, since no updates arrive while paused. The 19:37 crash (AV, rbx=-1) has not come back; the crash logger now prints real RVAs if it does.

### Note: Steam networking as transport (2026-10-04, user question)
- The game's own steam_api64.dll exports SteamNetworkingMessages v002, SteamNetworkingSockets v012, SteamMatchmaking v009 (lobbies), ISteamFriends invite overlay, and the old ISteamNetworking P2P. Our DLL runs inside the game process where Steam is already initialized with the game's own AppID 2552430, so no "Spacewar" (AppID 480) trick is needed or wanted: both players own the game.

UDP for one-PC tests (two copies share one Steam account, so Steam P2P can't connect them to each other) and Steam P2P (SendMessageToUser by SteamID, or a lobby + overlay invite) between real PCs. ## Session 1b (2026-10-04 evening, 1 hour): damage forwarding

Launch with `KH2COOP_PUPPET_COLLIDE=1` (hits on the copy were only seen with collision kept).

Both were blocked (Sora 2's HP unchanged by them) and forwarded as hit #1/#2; Sora 1's game applied them: 28→20→12. Sora 2 also took one genuine hit of his own at 04.714 (28→20), 19 ms after #1: the same swing caught both. No duplicates, no losses. Both games show the other's HP correctly.
- **Save guard (22:20):** `src/dll/save_guard.cpp`, env `KH2COOP_SAVE_GUARD=1` (launcher default). IAT hooks on the exe's KERNEL32 CreateFileW/CreateFileA/CopyFileA/DeleteFileA (the only file functions it imports, plus WriteFile on handles). Any path containing `\kingdom hearts hd 1.5+2.5 remix\steam\`: writes, copies and deletes go to `<dll folder>\save_sandbox_<pid>\`, reads go there if this session wrote that file. Backup before testing: `~/.universal-modder/backups/kh2-saves-before-saveguard/20261004-221902.zip`; real save SHA-256 before: KHIIFM_WW.png 4ca35cc6…, system 593ce383….

Restored from `kh2-saves-before-saveguard` at 22:24 while copy B was still running (Steam Auto-Cloud uploads on app exit), hash back to 4ca35cc6…; the bad state is kept in `kh2-saves-before-saveguard-pre-restore`. Then closed copy B.
  - Cause: the save isn't written through the exe's own KERNEL32 imports (likely via the CRT DLL, whose imports go to KernelBase). Volpestyle hooks KernelBase exports for exactly this reason.

- **Save guard v2 (22:29):** MinHook inline hooks on kernelbase CreateFileW/A, CreateFile2, DeleteFileW/A, MoveFileExW, MoveFileWithProgressW, ReplaceFileW, CopyFileW, CopyFileExW, CopyFile2 + kernel32 CopyFileA, MoveFileExA (all 13 ok). Every save-folder access is logged. **Coverage proven without saving:** a single guarded copy at the title logged the game's own startup reads of KHIIFM_WW.png, KH1.5+2.5_system_WW.png and config1525.dat (v1 logged 0). ## Session 2 (2026-10-04, 23:30)
- Fresh backup before the v2 save test: `~/.universal-modder/backups/kh2-saves-before-saveguard-v2/` (KHIIFM_WW.png 4ca35cc6…, system 593ce383…).

The user saved over slot 33 at 23:33:24: the guard caught it (`write …KHIIFM_WW.png -> …save_sandbox_2640\KHIIFM_WW.png`) and the **real save was unchanged** (still 4ca35cc6…). But the game showed "Error al guardar datos del juego": the sandbox stayed empty. Cause: the game saves a slot by opening the existing 7 MB file and overwriting part of it, so the open failed when no sandbox copy existed.
- **Fix (v3):** copy-on-write. The first redirected open for writing copies the real file into the sandbox; copy, move and replace targets are not seeded. Each redirected open logs access, disposition and ok/FAILED + error. Built; deploy once the game is closed, then repeat the save test.
- **Save guard v3 VERIFIED (23:36, pid 19212, user saved over slot 33 in Merlin's House, no error):** the first write open seeded the sandbox with a copy of the real file, then 4 opens (GENERIC_READ|WRITE, OPEN_EXISTING) all ok. Sandbox KHIIFM_WW.png is now 4747c3dd… (the new save); **real files unchanged** (KHIIFM 4ca35cc6…, system 593ce383…, config 0969dc99…). The game never reopened the file after saving: returning to the title and loading again logged no reads, so it keeps the save data in memory after startup. Consequence: saves made during a guarded session are lost when the game closes (the next start reads the real file). Fine for co-op tests; a "keep co-op progress" option would be a later user decision.
- Donald stayed in the party in both save tests: expected, these were single copies with only the guard on (no puppet / party patch).
- User decision (session 2): skip the title-menu crash until it reproduces; next is enemies targeting the Sora copy.

### Enemy targeting RE (session 2)
- Prior art: Volpestyle's docs have no enemy target-selection RE (only "enemies already target party members, so friend-slot puppets should work as aggro targets").
- Old logs (pids 18676/14364): with the Sora copy in the party, friend slot 1 `exe+0x2A239B0` held **Goofy** (its HP log is 30/30, Goofy's), and friend slot 2 `exe+0x2A239B8` was **empty**. The copy is in no friend slot.
- Static (tools/riprefs.py, new: finds RIP-relative refs to a range): only 15 instructions touch the friend slots, all in exe+0x3C2800..0x3C3500.
  - `exe+0x3C3270` GetFriend(i) = slots[i] (33 callers).
  - `exe+0x3D5C50` GetPartyMember(n): n=0 → player pointer at exe+0x2A105D0 (lead), else GetFriend(n-1). `exe+0x3D5C70` loops n=0..2 comparing exe+0x3B5B20(actor) (character id?) with an argument. **The game's party is {player, friend 0, friend 1}.**
  - Companion constructor (around exe+0x3C2E0B): first empty slot → slots[i] = actor, actor+0xDD0 = i, actor+0x6C8 |= 0x4000, actor+0x5C0 = stats from exe+0x3C02E0(charId) (likely why the copy shares Sora's HP: same character id). Destructors (exe+0x3C2870, 0x3C3470) clear slots[actor+0xDD0].
  - `exe+0x3C3390` zeroes both slots (one caller, exe+0x39CA46).
- Experiment `KH2COOP_CLONE_TARGET=1` (launcher passes it, default 0): when the copy appears, put it into the empty friend slot (prefers slot 1); take it out when it goes away or the room changes; never overwrite a non-empty slot; never write +0xDD0. `=2` also sets +0x6C8 bit 0x4000 on it. Logs both slots and +0x6C8/+0xDD0 of Sora, copy and friends before writing. Built.
- **Friend-slot write CRASHED (23:50, both copies; 23:53 single copy pid 25604):** right after "Sora copy put into friend slot 1". The pair run logged only a secondary stack overflow in clr.dll. The single run (crash logger now scans the whole stack on 0xC00000FD and sets a 64 KB stack guarantee) caught the real first fault: AV writing 0x8 at exe+0x1CD1D0 (`mov [rcx+8],rdx`, rcx=0), called from exe+0x1B5ECE. exe+0x1B5E30 (companion AI) copies {player, GetFriend(0), GetFriend(1)} into its table at exe+0xAD6A18..0xAD6A30 and links each friend's helper object `*(actor+0xDF8)` back to the actor; the Sora copy has none (0). That table is used only in exe+0x1B1000..0x1CC000 = companion AI, not enemy code. Flags seen: Sora and copy +0x6C8 = 0x01000087, Goofy 0x00004007 (bit 0x4000 = companion). Slot-write experiment removed.
- GetPartyMember's 13 callers (0x3ABA62, 0x3DBF39, 0x3DC203, 0x3DC2E2, 0x3DC859, 0x3F2A58, 0x3F3637, 0x3F6C6A, 0x4010C2, 0x40121A, 0x42FA1B…) loop n=0..2, call exe+0x3BA720(member) (validity check?) and test flags (+0x9B8, +0x6C8 bit 22). Can't tell statically which is enemy targeting.
- **Experiment v2, `KH2COOP_CLONE_TARGET=1`:** MinHook on GetFriend exe+0x3C3270. When slot 1 is empty and the copy exists, return the copy, except to callers in companion AI (0x1B0000..0x1CE000) or the party manager (0x3C2000..0x3C4000), or listed in `KH2COOP_CLONE_TARGET_SKIP=<hex rva>,...`. Each caller (return RVA) is logged on first use; per-caller counts every 1200 frames and on release.
- **v2 run (23:56, pid 9368, single copy):** loading the save no longer crashes. Callers given the copy (counts over ~10 s in Merlin's House): exe+0x3D5C98 (FindPartyMemberById loop) 48114, exe+0x400697 2376, exe+0x3F2A5D 1188, exe+0x40060B 1188; exe+0x1B5EB9 (companion AI) refused 594. Screenshot: a glowing cyan ring around the idle copy (unclear what it is; ask whether it was there before). HUD shows only Goofy's portrait.
- **Crash leaving Merlin's House (23:57:17):** AV reading 0x4C at exe+0x3B5B2C in GetCharId exe+0x3B5B20 (`handle = actor+8; rec = lookup exe+0x4AD270(handle); return rec->u16 +0x4C`), called from the 0x3D5C70 loop with the stale copy (handle 0, room already 0x09). Cause: g_clone stays set during loads (no entity updates). Fix: CloneAlive() = updated in the last 2 frames, same world/room, handle at actor+8 != 0.
- **v3 run (00:00, pid 8124):** room changes OK. Enemies still ignore the copy although GetFriend(1) now returns it to callers 0x3D5C98, 0x400697, 0x3F2A5D, 0x40060B, 0x3DBF3E, 0x3ABA67, 0x3DC208, 0x3DC2E7, 0x3A8837, 0x3ABBCD. The held copy took one incidental hit (same swing as Sora, blocked, no peer) and picks up HP orbs and munny as Sora (shared stats; later issue: in co-op the other player's copy would heal us). **Menu crash reproduced** at exe+0x36A4B2 right after the menu opened.
- **Menu crash explained:** exe+0x36A440 loops companion entries 1..3 of the menu party list; exe+0x2FC6D0(list, i) = s16 char id at list+i*0x20+0xA (1 Sora, 2 Donald, 3 Goofy); exe+0x3E60C0(id) (1..15 → id, 14→1, 15→6, else 0) minus 2 = companion index into per-companion ability bitfields (48 bits each). The copy has id 1 → index -1 → out-of-bounds read; intermittent because the first read sometimes lands on mapped memory. Callers of 0x2FC6D0 (25) don't all check 0, so "empty" isn't safe. **Fix:** exe+0x2FC6D0 re-implemented; companion entries with id 1 read as Donald (2) while the party patch is active.
- **v4 run (00:06, pid 23308):** menu opened many times in Merlin's House and outside: **no crash** (swaps logged for callers 0x30533E, 0x36A46A, 0x306643). The menu still shows Sora/Sora/Goofy (models and stats come from elsewhere). Target scan v1 (exact pointer or handle of Sora/companion in enemy+0..0x1000): **zero hits** in 13 reports. Crash ~1 s after entering room 0x08 (00:09:28): only a secondary stack overflow in clr.dll was logged (the .NET runtime's handler ran before ours); the stack leftovers contain exe+0x3B5B2C (GetCharId) x4, so possibly the stale-actor problem again, unproven.
- v5 (built): target scan v2 (every 15 frames; interior pointers into Sora/companion, handles with bit 31 masked, enemy + 64 child objects one level deep); crash logger re-adds its handler in front every 300 frames.

### Crash fixed: target scan v2 broke a thread's stack guard
- 14:50: crash 7 s after entering 04/09 (stack overflow at clr.dll+0x5F8BC0 probing its stack down to the limit; the exe is mixed-mode C++/CLI, imports mscoree). Cause: scan v2 read arbitrary child pointers with __try/__except; reading a guard page silently removes the guard a thread's stack needs to grow. Fix: `Readable()` (VirtualQuery: committed, readable, no PAGE_GUARD) before every child read. After the fix: several minutes among enemies, no crash (~26 child pointers per enemy skipped as unreadable). The 00:09 "room 0x08" crash in session 2 was probably something else (v4 had no child reads).

### Enemy targeting (static + live, this build)
- enemy+0x8F0 → Sora/Goofy+0x7B0 (first scan result) is **not** a target: actor+0x7B0 is the actor's node in a spatial grid; node+0x140 (= actor+0x8F0) is the "next" link, rebuilt every frame by exe+0x189810 (caught with the write watch, ~60 writes/s).
- **Game handles = compressed pointers**: exe+0x4AD390 encodes (bit 31 set, bits 25..30 index a 64-entry table of 32 MB-aligned bases at exe+0x2B0D720, bits 0..24 offset), exe+0x4AD3F0 decodes (0x4AD270 wrapper, 0x4AD240 encode wrapper). actor+8 is just the actor's own compressed pointer.
- Scan v3 (decodes every u32 handle): enemy+0xBF8 = compressed ptr to Sora in 129/129 scans; enemy+0xD38/+0xD40 = Goofy sometimes.
- **Write watch** (`KH2COOP_WATCH=BF8,D38,D40`, `src/dll/watch.cpp`): DR0..DR3 on one enemy's fields, armed from a helper thread with Suspend/SetThreadContext (setting DRs in a RaiseException context did not take effect). Writers:
  - enemy+0xBF8 by exe+0x3BE31F/0x3BE33F (0, then Sora), from exe+0x41C4FC (AI code in fn 0x41B400).
  - enemy+0xD38/+0xD40 by exe+0x411300 (Goofy), from the hit code (0x3E86E5 ← 0x410EF4 ← 0x3DBBCE).
- **exe+0x3BE2B0 SetTarget(slot = enemy+0xBF8, mode, actor)**; slot = {cptr target, u32 point, cptr, u32 mode}. Modes: 0 = player (exe+0x2A105D0) unless the 8-entry priority list at exe+0x2A16370 ({int prio, int, actor*} ×8, picked by exe+0x40C870: prio 0 wins at once, else lowest prio, random among ties) has a valid entry; 1 = last party attacker (enemy+0xD40) if valid and not +0x9B8 bit 2, else the player; 2 = player (0x3BEE60); 3/5 = nearest of the entities with +0x6C8 bit 4 (0x3BE7F0); 4 = 0x3BEA30; 6 = 0x3BE4B0 (range-weighted). Searches walk the active entity list (exe+0x3BF4F0: head exe+0x2A171C8, next = cptr at actor+0xA90), filter with exe+0x3BA260 (targetable: +0x9B8 bit 10, +0x120 bit 5 clear, has target points via +0xAB8) and exe+0x3BA900 (alive), and pick a target point (exe+0x3B4F10).
- **exe+0x411300 RecordAttacker(enemy, attacker)**: enemy+0xD38 = attacker; if the attacker is valid and team 1, enemy+0xD40 = attacker.
- HB Heartless use mode 0 (Sora) and mode 1 after being hit (that's how Goofy draws aggro). So **if the Sora copy's hits land locally, mode-1 enemies should switch to it**. Two-copy scripted test next.

### Two-copy scripted tests (session 3, 15:10-15:30)
- Two copies on the top monitor, both on virtual pads (`--vpad-a 27801 --vpad-b 27802 --width 960 --height 560`), both walked out by `tools/scene.py` in parallel. No human input.
- **Question (a) answered: the copy's attacks hit nothing.** Aggro log (`KH2COOP_AGGRO_LOG=1`, `src/dll/aggro.cpp`: pass-through hooks on RecordAttacker 0x411300 and SetTarget 0x3BE2B0) over a 30 s attack run by B: 0 RecordAttacker calls with the copy in A; all 14 enemy HP changes in A came from Goofy and **F_HB020 (team 3, HB's town defense turrets)**. My earlier reading ("-3/-2 hits while Goofy was KO = the copy") was wrong; those were turret hits. Mirrored motions don't create attack hitboxes.
- **Copy aggro experiment works** (`KH2COOP_COPY_AGGRO=1`, with the aggro log): an enemy whose target slot (enemy+0xBF8) holds Sora in modes 0-2 gets the copy when the copy is closer; checked in SetTarget and every 10 frames over the entity list (enemies mostly set a target once, when they appear). Fresh run 15:28: A's Sora drew the Heartless, then retreated ~850 units; in A, Sora→copy 20 times; mode-0 picks went to the copy; **the Heartless hit the copy 5 times in 9 s**, each forwarded to B and applied (B 28→20→12→4→0, Game Over in B). A's Sora took no hits after retreating. Symmetric in B (enemies retargeted to A's copy).
- Room-change crash (stale copy in GetFriend) happened again at 15:14 (A walked back into Merlin's House; GetCharId exe+0x3B5B2C, actor+8 = 0x01DC649D, not a handle). Fix: CloneAlive() also requires actor+8 to be a valid handle (bit 31 set, base in the table) and the actor still player-class. First try (actor+8 == own compressed pointer) was a wrong assumption: actor+8 is a handle to the character record, so it rejected the live copy too ("no copy" in the aggro log). ### N1 build (2026-10-05 evening)
- **Settings file:** `kh2coop.ini`, section `[kh2coop]`, keys = setting names without `KH2COOP_` (e.g. `PEER=`, `LISTEN=`). Looked up in the game folder first (survives OpenKH Build), then next to the DLL; environment variables override it (the launcher still uses them). Verified 19:59: a copy started with no KH2COOP_ variables, only the ini, came up with save guard, copy aggro, clone puppet and the link configured.
- **Link v2** (`avatar_link.cpp`): packets carry a build id (FNV of the compile date/time) and the sender's clock; other builds are ignored with one clear log line. `PEER=auto` (host): the friend's address is learned from its first valid packet. Binds 127.0.0.1 for a loopback peer, else 0.0.0.0 (`BIND=` overrides; expect a Windows Firewall prompt). SIO_UDP_CONNRESET off so "port unreachable" doesn't break receiving.
- **Network simulator** `NET_SIM=latency,jitter,loss%` on everything a copy sends.
- **Playback buffer** `INTERP_MS` (default 100): time-sorted history of the peer's poses (late/reordered packets kept), clock offset = min(local - sender) over 128 packets, the copy shows the pose from INTERP_MS before the newest, position/angle blended; motion/airborne from the older sample; no blending across rooms.
- **Copy aggro** (`COPY_AGGRO=1`) no longer needs the aggro log.
- `tools/netbench.py <mover port> <viewer port> [s] [-v]`: drives a square pattern, samples real vs copy position ~250 Hz. Results with NET_SIM=40,20,2: no buffer: lag 65 ms, **stalls 48%**, max jump 100/frame; buffer with GetTickCount64 clock: stalls ~6% (one repeated frame every ~0.4 s: 15.6 ms clock granularity); **buffer + QueryPerformanceCounter clock: lag 145 ms, error ~2 units, stalls 0%, max jump 10-16/frame**, both directions, 4 runs.
- Launcher: `--host-auto` (A = real host, PEER=auto), `--env-a/--env-b KEY=VALUE`; `tools/scene.py <port> bench [x z]` = load + outside + open street (square patterns near the door re-entered Merlin's House).
- `tools/package_friend.py <host ip>` → `dist/friend-pc/` (KH2 Coop mod folder, kh2coop.ini for the friend, INSTALL.txt). Host on the home Wi-Fi: **<host LAN IP>** (network profile Public: firewall prompt expected). Radmin VPN is installed (<host Radmin IP>): an option instead of Tailscale later.
- Host launch for a two-PC test (planned): `KH2COOP_COPY_AGGRO=1 KH2COOP_CLONE_TARGET=1 KH2COOP_PUPPET_COLLIDE=1 py tools/launch_pair.py --single --host-auto --pad-a 0` (window on the primary monitor).
- **Change of plan (user):** the second home PC is unavailable; a helper on another network will run the friend side over **Radmin VPN** (host Radmin address **<host Radmin IP>**). Package rebuilt: `py tools/package_friend.py <host Radmin IP>` (INSTALL.txt now covers Radmin, their own Steam copy, a Hollow Bastion save, firewall Allow on Public, and sending back their kh2coop_<pid>.log). Host firewall already has Allow rules for the KH2 exe (inbound TCP+UDP, Public profile); the Radmin adapter is Public.
- Save transfer: the friend's game rejects a copied save ("corrupted"): each container (PNG, sqEX chunk) has a 256-byte header XOR-keyed per account, row 0 = MD5("<SteamID64>1"). ## N2: host-led room following (2026-10-05, 22:00-22:12)
- `src/dll/follow.cpp`: friend side only (ROLE=host|friend, default host when PEER=auto; FOLLOW=1 default). When the host has an actor and has been in another world/room for 30 frames, and our game is safe (in field byte exe+0x9BA8D0 != 0, menu byte exe+0x7435D0 == 0xFF, frozen groups exe+0x2A171E8 == 0, Sora exists, Sora HP > 0, not title), call **exe+0x152990 RequestTransition(&{world, room, door, 0, map 0xFFFF, btl 0xFFFF, evt 0xFFFF}, 1, 0, 0, 0)** from OnFrame (game thread). Arrival = our NOW == target and in-field != 0; 15 s timeout then retry. Verified statically first (Ghidra: copies the 10-byte packet to NOW staging 0x717018 and starts the load task 0x152A90; the world-map exit 0x154E20 calls it the same way with programs 0xFFFF); prologue bytes checked before use. Lead: Volpestyle pointer_map_v1 "cold warp".
- **Bench results** (A host pid 26028, B friend pid 2132, scripted): host out of Merlin's House → friend loaded 04/09 door 02 in 50 frames (screenshot: normal Borough, fight in progress); host back in → friend followed in 46 frames, both at the same entrance spot (-605, 0, -450); friend walked out alone → waited for its own load ("room loading"), then pulled back to 0D in 50 frames; friend in the pause menu (menu byte = 0x0A) while the host changed rooms → "waiting: menu open" until closed, then followed in 47 frames. 0 crashes in both logs.
- Door after Game Over: **correction by the user** (my "exits lock during fights" was wrong): after Continue, Sora spawns standing inside the door's trigger, and the trigger only fires after leaving it and re-entering; a fight happened to start meanwhile. Expected game behaviour, nothing to fix. Scripts: walk well out of the trigger before re-entering.
- Not yet tested: world changes (world map), cutscenes (frozen-groups gate), the friend's party patch for a world it enters by following (patch applies "from the next room load").
- Package rebuilt after N2 (new build): helper needs the new KH2 Coop folder.
- **User decision (N2):** the friend must not go through any doors: lock the friend's own room changes (option b).
- **Door lock** (friend side, `LOCK_DOORS=1` default): MinHook on RequestTransition 0x152990; while the host is present (packet within 120 frames, has an actor) the friend's game may only load the host's room or reload its own room (Continue); anything else is refused and logged. Our follow calls go through the trampoline. Bench 22:20-22:25: friend at Merlin's House door with host inside → "refused our own move to 04/09"; friend stayed, screen normal (no stuck fade), could move and open/close the pause menu. Host went out → friend still followed (50 frames). Friend at the house door from outside → refused (22:23:19). **Side effect:** after one refusal the door never fired again during that room visit (the door script seems to wait for a load that never comes); following and normal play unaffected; the door works again after the next room load. Not tested: Continue (same-room reload) on the friend side; doors with the host gone (logic: no host packet for 2 s → nothing blocked).

## N3: shared world (2026-10-05, 22:40-23:05), bench only
User's scope: shared enemies AND companions (Goofy). Lessons from Volpestyle ENEMY_PARITY.md (leads): same room + battle program → same enemy set/order/spawn points; key = spawn point + object, never addresses; behaviour diverges at once, the host must own enemy state; continuous spawners diverge.
- `src/dll/world_sync.cpp` (`WORLD_SYNC=1` on both PCs). Host sends "KH2W" every frame (v2): header {build, seq, sender clock, world, room, count, companions, room instance, paused flag} + 32-byte entries {object id, net id, kind, flags, pos, angle, motion, HP} + enemy spawn spots. Enemy entries are captured right after the game updates each enemy (never re-read at send time).
- **N3a companions:** friend's companion with the same object id (Goofy P_EX030) skips its friend AI (FriendAI hook), motion only from us (MotionChainSet block), position/facing/velocity written after its update, HP copied (>0), local damage to it dropped. Bench: **Goofy mirror lag 120 ms, error 1.2 units, 0 stalls** (netbench --goofy).
- **N3b census:** both sides track team-2 actors per room with first-sighting spawn spot. The Borough uses **fixed spawn points**: first wave M_EX020 ×4 at (907,-160,-352), (501,-100,-484), (875,-100,50), (619,-100,-89) on both PCs; a second group M_EX010 ×3 at (-744,-1,-303), (-669,-301,-1682), (-419,-151,-1832) on both. Matched 4/4 and 3/3, spawn spots 0 apart.
- **N3c/d enemy mirror + claims:** friend binds each host enemy to a local one (same object, nearest spawn); bound enemies copy position/facing/motion/HP (no velocity writes on enemies: layout unverified); friend's damage to them is dropped and sent as claim "KH2E" (×3, dedup window); host applies via the game's HP change and calls RecordAttacker(enemy, friend's copy). Host enemy HP 0 or gone → local dies via the game's HP change. Unmatched locals parked 5000 below their spawn, killed 2 s after the host has no enemies. Friend's own Sora: local enemy hits dropped while the host's world is live (forwarded hits on the copy still apply).
- **Bench 22:59:** 4 Shadows bound 0 apart; friend's hits → host "friend hit our#2 M_EX020 for 7: HP 17 -> 10" (4 claims); all 4 host deaths → 4 friend deaths. 
- **Bug found and fixed (22:47-22:56):** (1) a dying enemy's freed stat record made every host packet throw for 11 s → each entry read separately, HP read only from committed non-guard memory; (2) during the host's Game Over the host kept sending stale enemy entries read from freed memory (pattern 0xEFEFEFEF = -1.07e29) and the friend wrote them into a bound enemy → entries cached at update time, "paused" flag when the host updated no entities this frame (friend then decides nothing), room instance counter (+1 when the in-field byte goes 0→1), friend rejects non-finite/huge positions.
- Host Game Over 23:02 (M_EX010 fight): friend held all decisions while paused. After Continue the host respawned at the door, its far-away enemies stopped updating, the friend killed its bound copy ("host's enemy is gone"). **Open:** Continue did not raise the room instance (in-field byte apparently stays set through Continue); harmless here (host net ids keep counting), but log/verify.

## Shared combat pause (user request, 2026-10-05 23:10-23:21)
- User/friend agreement: the in-combat pause ("PAUSE" only) is shared; the out-of-combat menu (equipment, items, party) stays local.
- **Verified live** (single copy, Borough fight): menu byte exe+0x7435D0 = 0xFF none, **0x0A full menu**, **0x08 combat pause**; **battle flag exe+0x2A11404** = 1 while Heartless are around, 0 otherwise; exe+0xABB878 = 1 during the combat pause (Volpestyle "pause blocker" lead).
- `src/dll/pause_sync.cpp` (`PAUSE_SYNC=1` both PCs): each side sends "KH2P" {paused, battle, epoch} every 10 frames and 3× on a change; epoch counts only the player's own changes (a change within 45 frames of our injected press is ours). Peer epoch moves on and differs from us → inject Start (raw slot 0 bit 0x0008 for 3 input reads): pause only when menu = 0xFF, battle != 0, in field; unpause only from 0x08; retry after 45 frames, max 3; otherwise log "not matching". 
- Input injection (`kh2coop.cpp`): `InputInjectEnable()` installs the collector hook in **pass-through** mode when no controller filter / virtual pad installed it (game input untouched, injected bits OR-ed into raw slot 0); `InputInject(bits, frames)`.
- **Bench (both copies on virtual pads, live M_EX010 fight):** A Start → B combat pause 36 ms later; A Start again → B resumed 134 ms later; B Start → A paused 36 ms; B again → A resumed 134 ms. First attempt after the fight had ended: Start opened the full menu (0x0A) on that side only, not shared (correct).
- Not tested: pass-through mode on a real PC (the friend's side), simultaneous presses.
- Two-PC host launch now: `KH2COOP_PAUSE_SYNC=1 KH2COOP_WORLD_SYNC=1 KH2COOP_AGGRO_LOG=1 KH2COOP_COPY_AGGRO=1 KH2COOP_CLONE_TARGET=1 KH2COOP_PUPPET_COLLIDE=1 py tools/launch_pair.py --single --host-auto --pad-a 0`.

## Two-PC test 3 (2026-10-05 23:27-23:30): world/pause sync silently off on the friend
- User saw: Goofy following both players after the friend loaded; enemies not synced after leaving Merlin's House; a fight the friend started was invisible to the host.
- Cause (friend log kh2coop_23696.log): new DLL (link build 2CF1C6C6, same as host) but the **old kh2coop.ini** (no WORLD_SYNC, PAUSE_SYNC, AGGRO_LOG) → no "world sync: on"/"pause sync: on" lines; Goofy ran his own AI there; each game ran its own enemies. My update notes had said only the KH2 Coop folder changed. Host log: 0 errors, 0 gaps/late, host opened one combat pause (not mirrored: friend's pause sync off).
- **Fix: feature handshake** ("KH2F" every 120 frames: WORLD_SYNC, PAUSE_SYNC, COPY_AGGRO, PUPPET, FORWARD_HITS, SAVE_GUARD bits); a difference is logged on both PCs as "SETTINGS DIFFER: X is ON here but off on the other PC". Bench 23:37 (B with WORLD_SYNC=0): both logs flagged it within 2 s. INSTALL.txt now says every update replaces BOTH the folder and kh2coop.ini.
- User proposal: force a shared combat status while either player fights. My view: the battle flag is a result of spawns, not their cause; forcing it on a game with no enemies wouldn't spawn anything and could leave that game stuck "in battle". The real gap is **spawning**: each game spawns encounters only where its own Sora walks, so a fight the friend starts alone exists only in the friend's game (with world sync it gets parked, so nobody gets it). Proposed next N3 step: **shared spawning** (whoever enters an area, the host's game spawns the encounter; the friend mirrors it), researched via the ARD spawn programs. Retest with the right ini first.

## Two-PC test 4 (2026-10-05 23:41-23:51), both on world + pause sync; fixes after it
User: improvements; enemies spawning at different moments on each PC (some die on one side right as the other spawns them, still showing the spawn animation); "shared spawning" needed. Friend logs: kh2coop_22168.log (crash), kh2coop_13472.log (second run). Both runs: same build, "the other PC runs the same co-op features as us".
- **Crash** (friend 23:45:12.333, exe+0x14F6B8 `mov [rdx+0x78], rax`, rdx = 0xEFACCAFEEFACCAFE freed-node pattern, stack 0x14FDFE 0x166571 0x1503BE 0x14F65B = task system): 12.197 host closed the combat pause; 12.264 world sync killed a local enemy (host's died) **from the frame hook while the friend's game was still paused**; 12.330 our injected Start closed the pause; 12.333 crash. Fix: kills are only marked in the frame hook and applied inside that enemy's own update (game live).
- **Reconnect** (host log): after the friend's restart its packet counter restarted at 1; the host counted every packet as "late" (+600 per 10 s, "received in order" frozen at 11522) and never used them. Fix: a counter jump back by more than 600 → "the other game restarted; starting fresh": link history/offsets/hit dedupe reset, world sync remote state and claim dedupe reset (friend unbinds), pause sync remote epoch reset; world packets accept a big seq drop too. Bench 23:53: "the other game restarted (packet 1 after 1524)", stream used again (0 late).
- **Spawn animation visible:** unmatched local enemies on the friend are now hidden at once (was 60 frames). Bench 23:55 then showed that killing hidden extras 2 s after the host has no enemies fires immediately when the friend reaches a group first; now a hidden extra is kept 15 s and removed only when the host has had no enemies for 5 s. Bench 23:57 (friend first): hidden 0.1-0.5 s, bound at 0 apart, 3 host deaths → 3 deferred friend deaths, no crash.
- Agreed next: **shared spawning** (one encounter in the host's game, whoever triggers it).

## Shared spawning: research (2026-10-06 ~00:00-00:30, no code) and plan for the next session
User asked for research only, so the next session can start implementing at once.

### Why encounters diverge (all three found in the game's code)
1. **Trigger boxes are checked against one position, the local player's.** Each game spawns a group only when *its own* Sora walks into the group's box.
2. **RandomSpawn / CasualSpawn** script commands (opcodes 2, 3) pick with the game's RNG (exe+0x783CA0, LCG x0x10DCD): two PCs can pick different groups.
3. **Enemy history** (recently defeated serials don't respawn for N room changes) is per PC.

### Data (verified by parsing `ard/us/hb09.ard` with a scratch script; matches the live spawns exactly)
- Room scripts `map`/`btl`/`evt` (BAR type 0xD); program = {u16 id, u16 len, ops}; op 0 `Spawn` lists spawn files by 4-char name (`b_00`...). OpenKH doc: docs/kh2/file/type/areadata.md. Research parser (outside the repo): `D:	ools\kh2-ghidra\scripts\spawnparse.py <unpacked .spawn files>`; unpack a room with `OpenKh.Command.Bar.exe unpack <ard> -o <dir>`.
- Spawn file (BAR type 0xC): {int 2, int groupCount}, then per group a 0x2C-byte descriptor {u8 **type** (2 = enemies; 1 entrance spawn, 3 event, 6..9 others incl. map change), u8 flag, u16 **id** (unique, e.g. 243..248), u16 entityCount, u16 activatorCount, u16 walkCount, u16 returnCount, int signalCount, ..., u8 area, u8 entrance, ...}, then entities (0x40 each: int objId, f32 x,y,z, f32 rot x,y,z, u8 spawnType @0x1C (2 = waits for its trigger, 0/1/3 spawn on activation), u8 spawnArg @0x1D (1 near player, 2 random in radius), u16 **serial** @0x1E, int arg1, arg2, ..., u16 delay @0x2A, ..., u8 wave @0x30), then activators (0x40 each: u16 shape, u16 option, f32 pos x,y,z, f32 scale x,y,z, rot, flags, type...), walk paths, return params, signals.
- hb09 b_00 group 2 (id 245) = the 4 Shadows we saw: M_EX020 at (907,-160,-352) serial 80 delay 25, (619,-100,-89) s79, (875,-100,50) s76, (501,-100,-484) s77; box centre (522,-128,-324) scale (590,139,604). Groups 0/1 = the M_EX010 at (-419,-151,-1832), (-669,-301,-1682), (-744,-1,-303).

### Code map (Ghidra + disassembly, this build; first bytes for hook checks)
| RVA | Role | First bytes |
|---|---|---|
| 0x3A2170 | RunRoomScripts(u16 programs[3]): for map/btl/evt finds the script (0x3A6480(ard at exe+0x2A0F828, 0xD, name)) and runs the program (0x3A23E0, dispatcher 0x3A24C0) | 48 89 5c 24 08 57 48 83 ec 20 f3 0f |
| 0x3A4E80 | RegisterSpawnFile(name4cc, alt): per group builds a 0x58-byte group object (ctor 0x3FDCE0) and appends {u32 name, u32 flags, ptr group} to the **group table exe+0x2A10010** (16-byte entries), count **exe+0x2A10418** | 40 55 56 41 56 48 83 ec 20 44 0f b6 f2 |
| 0x3FF550 | ActivateGroup(group) (room start, via 0x3A4E10 / 0x3A4D90): builds trigger-box objects (0x421010 per activator, list at group+0x10, next = handle at box+0x58), spawns spawnType 0/1/3 entities at once | |
| **0x3A4F90** | **SpawnTask** (per-frame task, registered 0x1506B0(1, 12000, 0x3A4F90)): actor = **exe+0x2A10420** (expected Sora; verify), pos = 0x3B5B40(actor); for every group with !(flags & 1): **GroupCheck(group, &pos)** | 48 83 ec 38 f3 0f 10 05 e4 ea 27 00 |
| **0x3FF000** | **GroupCheck(group, pos)**: clears flags bits 3-4; type-4 groups test a list of actors instead; else if **SpawnBlocked()** (0x3ABC80) == 0: group timer +0x20 -= frame delta (exe+0x717480); for each box: if **box->vtable[1](box, pos)** then flags |= 8 and **GroupTrigger(group, box)** | 48 89 5c 24 10 48 89 6c 24 18 57 48 83 ec 30 83 |
| **0x3FE320** | **GroupTrigger(group, box)**: switch on type: 2 = **GroupSpawnEnemies(group)**; 3 = event 0x3AABE0; 7 = 0x40C550 (map change); etc. **Only type 2 may ever be triggered for the friend.** | 48 89 5c 24 10 57 48 83 ec 40 48 8b d9 48 8b fa |
| **0x3FE6F0** | **GroupSpawnEnemies(group)**: per spawnType-2 entity: skip if FindActorBySerial(serial) or serial in history (0x3F5900) or entity wave > group wave (+0x2C); spawnArg 1 = near player, 2 = random in radius (0x3FE650), else **SpawnEntity**; group timer = entity delay; 0x3FEB50 advances waves | 48 8b c4 48 89 58 08 48 89 70 10 48 89 78 18 55 |
| **0x3FE590** | **SpawnEntity(record, group)**: pos/rot from the 0x40-byte record, actor = 0x3DF930(objId, &pos, rot), init actor->vtable[0x68/8](actor, obj, group, record), adds the serial to history for enemy-like object types | 48 89 5c 24 08 48 89 74 24 10 57 48 83 ec 30 f3 |
| 0x3ABC80 | SpawnBlocked(): exe+0x2A11400 bits 1/8, 0x3AC220(), 0x3A57F0(exe+0x2A10500) | 48 83 ec 28 8b 05 76 57 66 02 |
| 0x3B41E0 | FindActorBySerial(serial): walks the entity list; serial = u16 at (*(actor+0x9F0)) + 0x1E (actor+0x9F0 = its spawn record) | 40 53 48 83 ec 20 85 c9 bb 70 00 00 00 |
| 0x3F5900 | History contains(serial): struct exe+0x2AE5E60, array of 256 u16 at *(+0x820) + 8 | |

Group object (0x58): +0 name, +4 flags (1 done/disabled, 2 active, 8 triggered this frame), +8 descriptor, +0x10/+0x18 box list head/tail, +0x20 f32 timer, +0x24/+0x28 counters, +0x2C u8 wave, +0x30 entities, +0x38 activators, +0x40 walks, +0x48 returns, +0x50 signals.

### Verify first (read-only probe, ~10 min)
1. exe+0x2A10420 == the list head (Sora)?
2. box->vtable[1] takes a vec4 position (x, y, z, w) like the one 0x3B5B40 returns?
3. A live Borough Shadow's serial (u16 at *(actor+0x9F0) + 0x1E) == 80/79/76/77, the same on both copies.
4. The group table for hb09: names b_00..., descriptor types/ids as parsed.

### Implementation plan (one module, e.g. spawn_sync.cpp, under WORLD_SYNC)
1. **Host, the friend's position triggers spawns too:** hook SpawnTask; after the original, if the friend is in our room (fresh pose), for each group with descriptor type **2**, !(flags & 1), !(flags & 8) and SpawnBlocked() == 0: for each box, if box->vtable[1](box, &friendPos) then GroupTrigger(group, box). Same path and context as the game's own.
2. **Host announces spawns:** hook SpawnEntity; for type-2 groups send "KH2S" x3 {world/room, room instance, file name (group+0), descriptor id (desc+2), entity index ((record - entities ptr at group+0x30) / 0x40), serial, objId, spawn position}.
3. **Friend never spawns enemies on its own while the host is present:** hook GroupSpawnEnemies and return without spawning (host fresh, same room). Host gone: normal game.
4. **Friend spawns what the host announces:** queue it; in the SpawnTask hook (the game's own spawn context) find the group (table name + descriptor id), record = entities + index x 0x40; if FindActorBySerial(serial) == 0 then SpawnEntity(record, group). Bypasses RNG, history and waves on purpose.
5. **Bind by serial:** add the serial to world-sync enemy entries (packet v3); the friend binds host enemy and local actor with the same serial (record-less enemies keep the spawn-spot fallback). Parking stays for strays.
6. **Tests:** friend walks into the Shadows' box alone (host far away): host spawns, friend spawns the same 4 (serials 76/77/79/80), both see the fight. Then host first, simultaneous, a RandomSpawn room, deaths/claims/pause as before.
Risks: SpawnEntity outside the group's own flow (group timer/wave state on the friend untouched; watch battle end and barriers); spawnArg 1/2 enemies (positions are mirrored anyway); type-2 groups that drive missions via signals (test in a mission room later).

## Session 4 (2026-10-06): PS2-vs-PC check + verify-first probe
- **User question:** were last night's spawn IDs from the PS2 version? No. The spawn data was parsed from the PC install's own files (`...KINGDOM HEARTS openkh\data\kh2\ard\us\hb09.ard`, `00objentry.bin`, extracted by OpenKH from the Steam game), and every address came from disassembling this PC exe. Only the *file layout description* came from OpenKH's docs (areadata.md), which describe the Final Mix files that the PC port reuses; the live check below proves that layout holds on PC.
- **Verify-first probe** (`tools/peek_spawn.py`, read-only; one unattended copy, HB 04/09, pid 23372):
  1. exe+0x2A10420 == list head (Sora): yes.
  2. Box test exe+0x421180 takes `const float pos[4]` {x,y,z,1}: yes (disassembly; SpawnTask builds it from 0x3B5B40 and passes &local).
  3. Live Shadows: objId 0x12E M_EX020, serials 80/79/76/77 via actor+0x9F0 → record+0x1E, records 0x40 apart in file order. Group 245 flags 0x2 → 0xA (triggered) when Sora walked to (422,-476).
  4. Group table: 18 entries; b_00 type-2 ids 243..248 as parsed.
- Correction to the plan: SpawnTask's skip test is on the **table entry's** flags (entry+4, bit 1), not group+4.

## Shared spawning: implemented (2026-10-06, 17:00-17:41), bench passed
- `src/dll/spawn_sync.cpp` (on with WORLD_SYNC=1 unless SPAWN_SYNC=0; added to the feature handshake). Design changed from the plan: **no separate spawn announcements**. The world packet (now **v3**) carries each enemy's spawn serial, and the friend spawns every host enemy it lacks. That covers both "host spawned a group" and "friend arrives mid-fight" with one mechanism.
  - Host: hook SpawnTask 0x3A4F90; after the original, the same gates (in field, 0x3BA720(actor), !(actor+0x9B8 & 4), !SpawnBlocked) and then the friend's newest position (new `AvatarLinkPeerNow`, same room, < 0.5 s old) is tested against every type-2 group's boxes (skip entry flag 1, group flags 1|8); hit → group flags |= 8, GroupTrigger(group, box).
  - Friend: hook GroupSpawnEnemies 0x3FE6F0 → skipped while the host is in our room. In the SpawnTask hook: each host enemy with serial != 0 and HP > 0 that FindActorBySerial (0x3B41E0) doesn't find locally → its record in our type-2 groups (desc+0x2C+k*0x40, serial and objId match) → SpawnEntityAt 0x3FE650(record, group, {host pos, 0}) (the game's own spawn-at-position, used for spawnArg 1/2). Max 4 per frame; once per serial per epoch, again only after 5 s if ours is gone and the host still has it.
  - World sync binds by serial first (objId + nearest spot only when a side has no serial; never two different serials).
  - Found while decompiling: entity records live inline at **descriptor+0x2C** (not group+0x30); 0x3FE650 = SpawnEntity with an explicit position.
- **Bench (two copies, A host 16228, B friend 16548, HB 04/09):**
  1. Friend alone walks into the Shadows' box (host idle at the door): host "the friend at (1099,-160,-625) is in group b_00 id 245's box; triggering it"; friend "our group id 245 was triggered; the host's game spawns it instead" and spawned serials 80, 77, 76, 79 at the host's spots, **bound by serial, 0 apart**, in 0.45 s. Friend hits → host HP; host deaths → friend deaths.
  2. The idle host Sora was killed by those Shadows (Game Over; expected unattended). Continue: host enemies gone → friend's copies killed ("host's enemy is gone").
  3. Host walks into the box, friend outside it (friend's group flags stay 0x2): friend spawned all 4 again from the host's stream, bound by serial. Live positions matched within a few units.
  No exceptions in either log; real save folder unmodified.
- Friend package rebuilt (`py tools/package_friend.py <host Radmin IP>`, ini now has SPAWN_SYNC=1; dist/friend-pc.zip re-zipped). **Packet v3 + new build: the friend must update both the KH2 Coop folder and kh2coop.ini.**
- Not yet tested: both players entering a box at the same moment, a RandomSpawn room (opcodes 2/3; the friend now never rolls its own, so it should follow the host's pick), wave groups (0x3FEB50), mission/signal groups, spawnArg 1 "near player" enemies triggered by the friend (they appear near the HOST's Sora, mirrored to the friend), battle end / barriers on the friend.

## Manual test (user, two pads, 2026-10-06 17:45-17:51): host pid 11464 (slot 0), friend pid 7472 (slot 2)
- **Shared spawning worked in two rooms (04/09 Borough, 04/08):** the friend's position triggered 5 host groups (ids 245, 244, 243, 172, 173); the friend spawned 32 host enemies, **all 32 bound by serial 0 apart, 0 parked, 0 missing records**; 73 friend hit claims applied on the host; 24 mirrored deaths. No crash, no exceptions.
- **Pause sync bug: a Game Over counts as a combat pause.** The Game Over screen sets the menu byte exe+0x7435D0 to 0x08, the same value as the combat pause. Proof without any input: unattended bench 17:38:55 host Sora HP 0 → 17:38:59 host "our player opened the combat pause" → friend paused to match until the host pressed Continue (17:39:55). I missed this when reading the bench logs. In the manual test: friend HP 0 at 17:50:04.7 → 17:50:08.8 friend "opened the combat pause" → host paused 36 ms later; the user pressed Start on the host (17:50:11.6, "our player closed"); the friend then pressed Start 3 times trying to leave its "pause" (Game Over can't be closed by Start) → "gave up ... (menu 0x08)". Friend Continue 17:50:22 → "closed" sent; host already running. Harmless this time, but one player's Game Over freezes the other's game.
- Proposed fix: pause sync treats menu 0x08 with our Sora at HP 0 as Game Over, not a pause: don't announce it, and don't press Start in our game while we're at Game Over. The other player keeps playing.

## Downed instead of dead (user design, 2026-10-06 17:55-18:31)
User: the friend's Sora is a member of the host's party, so its death should work like a knocked-out companion (plan said 20 s down; unsure of the game's own time). Host death: whatever is easier (chose: same rule both ways; Game Over only when both are down).
- **Game's own companion KO rule (measured, the web had nothing):** new test command `tools/debug_cmd.py <port> damage <who> <amount>` (copy started with `KH2COOP_DEBUG=1`, loopback only; who 0 Sora, 1/2 companions, 3 the other player's Sora copy) deals damage through our ApplyStatDelta hook like an enemy hit. Writing HP 0 directly does nothing (no KO): only the game's HP change triggers it. Goofy/Donald KO'd: motion 54 (fall), out of a fight up after **5.7 s**; in a fight they stayed down **≥27 s** while it lasted, and got up **~6-7 s after the fight ended** (battle flag exe+0x2A11404 → 0), with **max/4 HP** (Goofy 7/30, Donald 5/21), get-up motion **55**.
- **Sora's motions differ:** 54 is a ~22-frame collapse that loops (bent forward, never lying); Sora has **no 55**. Asking SetMotion for a motion Sora lacks → **T-pose** (verified by screenshot). After 54 the game never picks Sora's next motion itself: stuck, stick ignored, until something sets one (SetMotion 0 frees him; he then runs normally). Sora's valid motion ids (enumerated with `debug_cmd.py motion`): 0, 3-8, 10-19, 40-43, 52, 54, 56-65, 67, 68, 70-73, 130, 137, 138, 151-155, 161-167, 169-173, 181-184, 191-194, 196, 197, 201-209 (1/2 missed by the readback timing; 9 skipped, known crash).
- `src/dll/downed.cpp` (DOWNED=1 default, DOWN_SECONDS=20; in the feature handshake). Own Sora's lethal HP loss (game hits via the ApplyStatDelta hook, forwarded hits in the pending loop) while the other player is in our room, alive and not down → HP left at 1, state Down: input blocked except Start (new `InputBlock`, raw slot 0), all HP loss on our Sora dropped, motion 54 set (our call) with the game's motion changes blocked and the clock held at frame ~18 (no loop). Up after DOWN_SECONDS or 360 frames after the battle flag drops: HP → max/4, motion 0, control back 10 frames later. Other player down/dead while we're down → our Sora dies through the game's HP change (Game Over both). Room load / new Sora actor → up. Avatar packet flag 4 = downed; PeerPose now carries downed + hp.
- **Pause sync fix:** menu 0x08 with Sora HP 0 (in a world) = Game Over, not a pause: not announced, never pressed against.
- **Double damage fix (found in this bench):** the friend's game forwarded hits on the host's copy from its mirrored enemies, so the host was hit by its own enemy AND the mirror. Now the friend doesn't forward hits on the copy while the host's world is live (WorldSyncHostPresent).
- **Bench (two copies, DEBUG=1):** (1) friend down in Merlin's House: HP 1, held collapse, up after 6.0 s with 7/28, runs afterwards. (2) host down alone: stick ignored while down, up after 6.0 s with 7/28, moves after. (3) host went down from real Shadow hits, then friend hit for 999 → friend died (partner down), host "both players are down: Game Over"; no pause messages on either side. (4) friend down in a fight: up 6 s after the fight ended (17.6 s down). No exceptions; real save untouched.
- **Open:** the downed pose is a placeholder (Sora bent forward mid-collapse, no get-up animation); a real lying pose needs a motion from Sora's set (candidates 10-19 knockdown reactions, not yet identified: the camera view was unreadable). 20 s cap during a long fight not hit yet (fights ended first). Enemies keep attacking a downed player's copy (hits are dropped). Reviving early with Cure/items not done.
- Friend package rebuilt (ini: DOWNED=1, DOWN_SECONDS=20). Friend must update folder + ini again.

## Mickey rescue research (user idea, 2026-10-06 18:35-18:52): Sora's real downed + revive animations
User: Sora has no get-up motion of his own, but when he dies in some boss fights a white screen ("¡No me rendiré!" / "Todo ha terminado...") lets Mickey come and revive him; Sora plays a revive animation. Could we use it, and force Mickey in a test to log it?
- **Which fights:** mission file flag 0x200 "Is Mickey spawnable" (OpenKH docs/kh2/file/type/msn.md). PC files: 11 missions have it: AL03_MS103, BB05_MS104A/B, BB11_MS102, BB15_MS203, CA18_MS202, EH24_MS110, HE07_MS103, HE18_MS108, MU08_MS204, MU09_MS108. Borough fights have no mission (HB09_MS101/901 flags 0x1A1, no 0x200).
- **Code (this build):** GameOver ctor **exe+0x3FC7A0** (instance ptr exe+0x2AE8050; +0x10 state, byte +0x14 = Mickey chosen). Mickey only if **MickeyAllowed exe+0x3A3BD0** (mission ptr exe+0x2A0FF68 → +8 header → u16 flags @+4 bit 9) and no active companion fails exe+0x3C3950, then chance = max(pow(base, count exe+0x9AD660), min) vs the LCG (exe+0x783CA0). Counter exe+0x9AD660/0x9AD662 = Mickey appearances (save data; 999 cap). Mickey actor created at exe+0x4158C0 (vtable 0x7523B8 class YS::MICKEY), which calls **exe+0x3C6CC0(motion bank, Sora, 0xFC, 0)**: plays motion 252 on Sora from an external bank (sets actor+0x2B8 = bank, actor+0x180 = id). White-screen callback exe+0x3FCC50 loads **obj/GAMEOVER.mset** (exe+0x39E000 loader, pool 5000); exe+0x3FD2B0 looks it up (0x39CC40).
- Leftover developer debug menu "field" in the PC exe (name table exe+0x749A90): anytime drive, anytime magic, r2 map debug, skip title, ignore zone, test limit, exception, gm save local, stop enemy, demo movie, infinity item, copyright, onioshi, clear cache, **no gameover**, **anytime mickey**, free ability, gentle friend, ... (flag storage not yet found).
- **Forced test (single test copy pid 21952, Borough door, no enemies):** patched 0x3A3BD0 → `mov al,1; ret` in memory, appear count 2 → 0, Sora killed with debug_cmd. Result: white screen appeared; "¡No me rendiré!" → Mickey (P_EX200) becomes the list head/player, Donald and Goofy removed. Trace (30 Hz, scratch `mickeytrace.py`):
  - Sora: 54 (collapse) → **252 = lying on the ground with stars** for the whole rescue → Mickey's Healing Light (Mickey motion 192 ~2.9 s; charge = 193, ~2 s per press, ~20 presses with no enemies) → Sora HP 28/28 and **253 = getting up (4.8 s)** → 6 → 0, control back; Donald/Goofy respawn.
  - After the sequence, SetMotion 252/253 on Sora is rejected: they come from the external bank (GAMEOVER.mset), not Sora's own set.
- **Feasibility for the downed state:** good, not trivial. Needed: load GAMEOVER.mset ourselves (the game's loader), get/build a motion bank for it like the Mickey object's (+0x158), then exe+0x3C6CC0(bank, Sora, 252/253). RE still to do: how that bank is built, and that a later real Game Over (which loads the same file into pool 5000) doesn't clash.

## Downed state uses the Mickey-rescue motions 252/253 (2026-10-06 18:55-19:02), build ready for the user's test
- **Where they live:** motions 252/253/254 exist only in Mickey's set **P_EX200.mset** (entries 1008/1012/1016 = motion*4, type **20**: a small BAR of 12 per-character sub-motions in groups "0010"/"0011"/"0012" × 4 variants). Bone count (u16 at motion+0xA0) tells them apart: Sora = 0xE4 (228), Mickey = 0xC6; 252 → group 0010 sub 2 (Sora), 253 → 0010 sub 1 (Sora) + 0011 sub 5 (12 bones, likely the Keyblade). Lookup exe+0x3C7C50(motCtrl, id): index = id*4 + variant (variant order from a table by state), secondary set first, then primary; -1 if none.
- **`tools/make_mset.py`** builds Sora's set with Sora's two sub-motions added as plain type-17 entries at slots 1008/1012 (DUMM padding, data shifted, round-trip checked: the 839 original entries byte-identical). Source = this PC's OpenKH extraction; output build/mod_assets/obj/P_EX100.mset (1013 entries). `openkh_mod/mod.yml` now also copies it to obj/P_EX100.mset; deploy.py builds it and installs it in the mod source and the built output (mod/kh2/obj/); package_friend.py ships it in "KH2 Coop" (private test between game owners; a public release must build it on the player's PC). No other enabled mod touches any .mset.
- **Verified (single copy pid 20144):** Panacea serves the new file: SetMotion 252/253 on Sora now read back 252/253 (before: rejected / T-pose); screenshots: 252 = Sora lying flat, 253 = rising; after 253 the game itself moves to motion 6 (as in the rescue). The stars effect over his head did not show (likely the rescue's own effect, not in the motion).
- **DLL:** `PuppetHasMotion` (exe+0x3C7C50 on actor+0x158, bytes checked) guards every rescue motion. downed.cpp: down → 252 held (re-set if anything changes it), no clock pin; revive → HP max/4 + 253, still protected and input-blocked until 253 ends (motion changes, clock wraps, or 330 frames) → idle → control back 10 frames later. Fallback when Sora's current set lacks 252/253 (Drive forms, other worlds' Sora variants, a PC without the file): the old collapse-54 pose and plain stand-up. Puppet copy: a peer motion > 200 the copy's set lacks → idle (no T-pose on the other screen).
- Friend package rebuilt (KH2 Coop now has kh2coop.dll + mod.yml + P_EX100.mset; friend must Build in Mods Manager). Not yet tested in a two-copy downed run (user will test).

## User test 3 (19:18-19:22; host 28624, friend 14160) and Keyblade timing (19:23-19:31)
- Logs: friend down 19:20:11 → up 19:20:30 (fight ongoing); **aggro fix works**: host "enemies moved off a downed player so far: 4" (friend side 14, mirrors). Camera stick while downed: user did not report a problem.
- User: the Keyblade animation happened "way earlier" than in the rescue; ideally on the last second of the get-up.
- **Why:** 253's trigger data (type 16 in the anb, OpenKH docs/kh2/file/anb/anb.md "Motion Triggers"): ranges 0..-1 ids 44/29/27, attack box 10 (124,6), 4 (15) 0..238, 23 fades; **frames: 238 id 29 "weapon appears with effect"** + 238 id 14 voice (VSB 28), 120 id 14 voice 7, 120 id 8 sound (594,60), 234 id 2 footstep. 253 = 288 game frames (30 fps LFR, end 144 → 4.8 s), clock actor+0x19C counts game frames, so 238 ≈ 3.97 s. In the rescue the weapon was put away before; ours was out in a fight (the game draws it in battle), so it showed from frame 0.
- **Code:** frame-trigger dispatcher exe+0x40DD70 (switch on trigger id, jump table at 0x40DD95): 22 → exe+0x3D7DB0 (weapons appear), 29 → **exe+0x3D6A90(owner)** (each weapon: exe+0x3EFD70, flash + show), both only if owner+0x6C8 bit 2. Owner weapons at actor+0xD60/+0xD68. Weapon handler (decode(*(u32*)weapon)) vtable (Keyblade 0x5C9408): +0x80 = put away (flags +0x124 |= 0x700), +0x88 = show (clears 0x100, 0x400). 0x400 set = away (verified: idle in Merlin's House flags 0x700, after an attack 0x0).
- **Fix (downed.cpp):** while down and during 253 before clock 238, put our Sora's weapons away through the handler's +0x80; done after each weapon's own update (the weapon's update shows it again while it has the owner's motion: first try, hiding only in Sora's update, left it out). From 238 the game's own trigger brings it back; fallback: exe+0x3D6A90(Sora) if still away 6 frames later (logged).
- **Bench (two copies, friend downed in the Borough with the Keyblade out):** away from the down at 0.15 s through 253 clock 200, **OUT exactly at clock 238** (the game's trigger; the fallback never ran), then 6 → 0. No exceptions. Friend package rebuilt.

## Session 6b (2026-10-06 21:00-21:58): Magic/Fusion "skipped" fix, healing while downed (bench; user to test orbs by hand)
User: do healing while downed first, in Merlin's House; items used on the copy heal the user's own Sora, and Cure doesn't target the copy. Then: "you skip Magic and Fusion", fix that first.

### Magic and Fusion skipped in the command menu (fixed)
- Not greyed: the menu's own entry states said all four commands enabled. **Every player-class actor's update runs the shared field command menu** (YS::FIELD_COMMAND at exe+0x2A10620; each player's +0xDC0/+0xDD0 point at it, set by the player ctor exe+0x3A7A40) through exe+0x3FD690 -> 0x3B2340 (input, cursor, timers). With the Sora copy it ran twice per frame (research hook on the input step 0x3F2470: once in Sora's update, once in the copy's, same callers 0x3BFEBE -> 0x3FD6C0 -> 0x3B2530), so one down moved two entries (Magic and Fusion looked skipped) and cross also picked the submenu's first entry (Items went straight to the PARTY target list). Reproduced with our code off: party table patched by hand in a PUPPET=0 copy, game-spawned copy -> same skip.
- Fix (puppet.cpp): hook exe+0x3FD690; skipped while the actor being updated (tracked in the PerEntityUpdate hook) is a player-class actor other than Sora. Bench: down x3 = Magic -> Items -> Fusion.
- Side finds: pad state exe+0xBF3270 (GetPadState exe+0x39B760; +0 held, +2 stick directions, +0x20.. floats; blanking it during the copy's update did NOT stop the menu: the menu reads its own input); Axa::DebugPad at exe+0x7133E0; debug switch word exe+0x749804 (bit 0 anytime drive, 1 anytime magic, 4 ignore zone; bits 0/1 have no readers in this build); command availability exe+0x401D60 (per command type), sub-list greying in exe+0x3B0F20.
- Also seen by accident: the host drove into Valor Form with the copy present and reverted: no crash.

### Healing while downed (user decision: only Cure/items revive; HP orbs do nothing)
- How heals reach ApplyStatDelta (return addresses): Cure and other heal "attacks" via the hit handler exe+0x3D60C0 (returns to 0x3D613C); items via exe+0x3C4B80 -> 0x3C0C30 (returns to 0x3C4C5E); HP orbs neither. Cure heals Sora +44 and Goofy +22 (party manager 0x3C34D0); the copy isn't hit.
- Items: the menu target list (CLONE_TARGET=1) shows Sora, Goofy, Sora(copy). The menu's execute step **exe+0x3B22D0(menu, command, target info)** still has the target (info +0 = actor handle, exe+0x3BEE60); the item use it leads to (record built by the motion trigger 0x40DFEA -> 0x3F6650: +4 item id, +8 USER actor, +0x10 user char, +0x14 target char) only keeps character id 1, which is why the copy's item landed on our own Sora. Item table *(exe+0x2A25370): 0x18-byte rows, u16 id, u16 power (% of max HP) @+4; ids 1 Potion, 2 Hi-Potion (Ultrapocion here, 60%), 3 Ether, 4 Elixir, 5 Mega-Potion, 6 Mega-Ether, 7 Megalixir (5/6/7/0x83 whole party, exe+0x3C4AB0).
- Code: downed.cpp DownedInterceptHeal (down: Cure/item -> get up now with 1 + heal, at least max/4; anything else dropped and logged "does nothing while down"). puppet.cpp: heal source from the stack; a Cure on our Sora with the copy within 500 units (or with the copy chosen as Cure's target) sends the heal to the other player; a command executed on the copy (0x3B22D0 hook) makes its item land on the other player instead of our Sora (0x3F6D00 hook); party items also heal the other player. New packet "KH2R" (heal, % of max HP; "KH2E" was already world sync's claim packet). Heals arriving for a player who is up just heal.
- Bench (two copies, Merlin's House, CLONE_TARGET=1): host Hi-Potion on the copy -> friend down 2.5 s got up at once with 18/28, host not healed. Host Cure (0 away) -> friend up with 28/28. Host Cure 931 away -> "did not reach", friend stayed down. Borough fight: HP orbs on host and friend log "an HP gain (orb...)" (the class that is dropped while down). Down + orb not reproduced unattended (the fight ended first); **user tests it by hand** (start_pair.bat).
- `start_pair.bat` (project root): two copies, host pad 0 / friend pad 2, host settings + DEBUG=1.
- Friend package NOT rebuilt yet for these changes (do after the user's test).

### Limit soft lock: cause and fix (bench + user test 22:35-22:56)
- Bench (one copy, OC save index 31 -> 06/06 Hades scene -> 06/0F, Large Body M_EX050 of group 62): Bushido = **reaction command type 0x51** (ids 0x52 start, 0x53/0x80/0x54/0x81/0x55 the follow-ups). Trace: Sora, the copy AND Auron all got motion 253 with the Limit's bank (actor+0x2B8 != 0) in the same frame; then the copy + Auron (teleported next to the copy at the room entrance) played 254-257 while Sora stayed in 252. User test with the copy hidden from GetFriend at the start (CLONE_TARGET_SKIP=3D8BFC): same; "whole Limit" done but no damage, Sora invincible and stuck.
- Limit motions come from `limit/<lang>/auron.bar` ('LIMI' set: entries 252..258, type 20, groups 0010 Sora / 0011 Keyblade / 0050 Auron / 0051 sword). Reaction-command motions come from the enemy's set (type 20 at 63+: index 252.. too). So **motion ids with a bank are indices in another file's set and overlap Sora's ids** (our rescue 252/253).
- Live current-Limit pointer **exe+0x2A24CC0** (object 0x128 bytes: +0 limit record handle, +0xC user handle, +0x10/+0x14 partners): user = **the copy**. Limit start exe+0x3D8B40(limit id, user) is reached only from **exe+0x405440(actor, command {u16 id, u16 type})**, a player actor carrying out a picked command (0x51/0x2C3 -> Limit with id at *(actor+0xDD0)+0xA; 4, 5, 0x48, 0x49, 0x13C; else exe+0x3A8B10); vtable slot at .rdata 0x5CBB60 (thunk exe+0x404FD0). It ran for Sora and for the copy (shared command menu at +0xDD0): two Limit starts per press (GetFriend(1) from 0x3D8BFC twice), the second (the copy's) replaced Sora's.
- **Fix (puppet.cpp HookPlayerCommand):** exe+0x405440 skips any player actor that isn't the list head. User retest 22:54: "the Limit command works correctly"; trace: Sora + Auron 253 -> 252 -> 254/255/256 -> 257 at Sora's spot; log "the Sora copy carries out command 0x52 (type 0x51): skipped". The reaction command on the Large Body (0x25) was doubled too (now skipped on the copy). User also cast Fire/Blizzard and did a reaction command: fine. The Limit gauge (top right) barely moved: exe+0x3D9130 only lowers it while Sora and the partners have +0x194 bit 0 clear (likely mid-attack), not compared with an unmodded run yet.
- Also found: GetFriend(i) = u64 slots exe+0x2A239B0; exe+0x3C3280(char) = friend with that character (slots only); exe+0x3D5C50(n) = party member n (0 player, else GetFriend(n-1), tail jump: GetFriend's return address is 0x3D5C50's caller); exe+0x3D5C70(char) = first party member with that character.
